- **a11y**: Accessibility auditor for UI code. Use when reviewing components, pages, or user flows for WCAG 2.2 AA/AAA compliance — typically before shipping a UI feature. Covers semantic HTML, keyboard navigation, ARIA, forms, focus management, color contrast, and motion. Framework-aware (React, Vue, WordPress blocks, vanilla HTML). Reports findings by severity with file:line references and concrete fix suggestions. ([stuartshields/claude-setup](https://github.com/stuartshields/claude-setup/blob/HEAD/agents/a11y.md) · ★2)
- **a11y**: Guidance for creating more accessible code ([ucsb/drupal-site-test-suite](https://github.com/ucsb/drupal-site-test-suite/blob/HEAD/examples/drupal-ai/agents/a11y.agent.md) · ★2)
- **a11y**: Combined accessibility audit lead and read-only evidence reviewer. Runs the full accessibility lifecycle for a component or page — scan, deep review, remediation, verification, and regression-test generation — and produces a consolidated WCAG 2.1/2.2 AA report. Orchestrates the accessibility skills. ([deepakkamboj/developer-assistant-skills](https://github.com/deepakkamboj/developer-assistant-skills/blob/HEAD/.developer/agents/a11y.agent.md) · ★1)
- **a11y**: Accessibility audit and fix agent for F1 Prediction. Use when auditing components for WCAG 2.1 AA compliance, fixing ARIA issues, ensuring keyboard navigation, managing focus, or verifying bilingual accessibility (all aria-label/alt text must use t() from useLanguage). ([fedesanchezvidarte/f1-prediction](https://github.com/fedesanchezvidarte/f1-prediction/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/a11y.md) · ★1)
- **a11y**: Audits and improves WCAG 2.1 AA accessibility — ARIA patterns, keyboard navigation, semantic HTML, color contrast, and focus management. ([splits-network/splits](https://github.com/splits-network/splits/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/a11y.md) · ★1)
- **a11y**: Automated accessibility audit agent. Three-phase pipeline: (1) axe-core rule-based scan via @axe-core/playwright (covers ~35% WCAG 2.1 AA issues), (2) semantic layer grouping violations by WCAG POUR principle with user impact and code-level fix suggestions, (3) AI-generated alt text for images lacking descriptions. Produces a structured report with WCAG SC references, severity, and fix confidence. Use when asked to "qa accessibility", "a11y audit", "WCAG check", "axe scan", or included automatically by /qa-team for web apps. ([vslvslv/agentic-qa-team](https://github.com/vslvslv/agentic-qa-team/blob/HEAD/agents/a11y.md) · ★1)
- **a11y**: Use when UI has been added or changed and you want concrete accessibility defects found — keyboard traps, missing labels, bad focus, weak contrast. ([Yazeed-Alotaibi/claude-config](https://github.com/Yazeed-Alotaibi/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/a11y.md) · ★1)
- **a11y**: Accessibility (a11y) specialist for React/Tailwind applications. Use when changing UI, adding new components, or when accessibility review is requested. Checks semantic HTML, ARIA attributes, keyboard navigation, contrast, and live regions. ([elyashenko/sse-demo](https://github.com/elyashenko/sse-demo/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/a11y.md))
- **a11y**: You are an accessibility specialist reviewing the Pixels and Code portfolio website. Your role is to ensure the site is usable by everyone, regardless of ability or assistive technology. - **a11y-adversary**: Adversarial reviewer for the accessibility claims nemik's web view (src/nemik/web/index.html) and its browser tests make. Invoke it when a view change touches colour, halos, chips, labels, legends, layout floors or interaction, and before an a11y claim is cited anywhere. Its mandate is to REFUTE a claim, not to confirm it. Read-only; it reports attacks, it does not edit. - **a11y-adversary**: Adversarial reviewer for paperkit's render + accessibility-conformance claims. Invoke it whenever a render/a11y capability or a WCAG/508/EN "Supports" verdict is added or changed, and BEFORE any conformance disclosure is trusted. Its mandate is to REFUTE — to find a false Supports, an unsound entailment, an inaccurate standards mapping, or an overclaim — not to confirm. Read-only; it reports refutation attempts, it does not edit. ([mikemol/paperkit](https://github.com/mikemol/paperkit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/a11y-adversary.md))
